2  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/
上週重要市場資訊
⚫ 美國 9 月通膨數據高於市場預期，CPI 月增 0.6%、年增 3.7%；核心 CPI 月增
0.3%、年增 2.6%。同月 PPI 月增 1.3%，創 2022 年 3 月以來最大增幅，年增
6.3%， 顯示通膨壓力已逐步由能源價格擴散至更廣泛領域 。 數據公布後 ， 市場一
度提高對聯準會 10 月升息的預期，但隨後多位官員釋出審慎訊號，使升息押注
降溫 。 聯準會理事鮑曼表示 ， 決策前仍有時間觀察先前升息效果 ； 舊金山聯準銀
行總裁戴莉則認為 10 月無立即升息之必要。而褐皮書顯示，美國就業市場維持
「低招聘、低裁員」格局，而高能源價格帶來的通膨壓力仍值得關注。
⚫ 中國 9 月通膨持續回升，CPI 年增 0.9%，創近三個月高點；PPI 年增 5.0%，創
逾四年最大漲幅，顯示通縮壓力進一步減輕。主要受能源與原物料價格上漲帶
動，且 9 月進出口表現優於預期，AI 產業鏈需求與貿易環境改善提供支撐；不

過，信貸增長仍仰賴政府發債與季底效應，內需與民間融資動能仍偏弱。
⚫ 日本銀行審議委員小枝淳子表示 ， 不宜對升息過於保守 ， 並認為政策利率仍有進
一步上調空間。總裁植田和男則強調維持2%通膨目標的重要性。整體而言，日
本央行持續釋出偏鷹派訊號，有助支撐日圓表現。
⚫ 歐洲央行管委溫施表示 ， 在能源價格波動下 ，為了控制通膨仍不排除進一步升息
可能，總裁拉加德則認為法國債務問題應透過財政政策解決， 歐洲央行官員普遍
認為能源價格的潛在波動依然是推升通膨並使其居高不下的主要風險來源。。
⚫ 澳洲央行會議紀錄指出，能源價格上漲與需求韌性可能使通膨更具黏性，保留
進一步升息空間。然而 9 月就業數據意外疲弱，市場大幅下調升息預期，目前
預估 11 月維持利率不變的機率接近 90%。

### Page 3

3  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/
美元兌新台幣匯率走勢
Oct/19/2026
＜上週走勢＞
上週台幣匯價高點為 31.890，低點在 32.520，以 32.338 作收，較前一周收盤價
貶值 4 角 6.3 分。
上週台幣走貶 ， 周初 ， 外資延續前周大幅匯出的走勢 ， 雖逢高吸引出口商拋匯 ，
仍不敵外資匯出力道令台幣走貶 ； 週二台股重挫近千點 ， 外資大幅賣超台股1653 億
並匯出，台幣接連跌破 32.000、32.100 及 32.200，總成交量 71.3 億，創歷史新高；
周三台股收漲，外資雙向進出，台幣收斂部分跌幅；週四，受美國通膨高於預期影
響，早盤台幣跳空開低，台股跌逾2%，外資大幅賣超 3025 億，台幣跌破 31.400 關
卡 ， 總成交量91.2 億 ， 再創歷史新高 ； 週五外資持續匯出 ， 台幣貶至周內低點32.520，
終場在央行調節之下收在 32.338。
＜未來分析＞
預估本週台幣走勢偏弱。就國際市場而言，美國 CPI、PPI 雙雙高於預期，通

膨壓力仍在，儘管聯準會官員陸續發言表示不急於升息，淡化市場對於聯準會十月
升息預期，惟年底前聯準會仍有升息一碼空間，利率持穩於高位將給予國際美元支
撐；就台灣市場來看，市場對AI 產業資本支出、融資成本及信用風險產生疑慮，若
賣壓延續，將壓抑市場風險偏好，增加外資調整台股部位及匯出的壓力，令台幣面
臨持續貶值的壓力，惟出口商拋匯將抑制台幣貶值速度，綜合以上，預估本週台幣
走勢偏弱。
＜預估本週＞ 美元兌台幣：32.100-32.700

### Page 4

4  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌人民幣匯率走勢  
Oct/19/2026 
＜上週走勢＞ 
上週人民幣震盪走升。上週人民幣中間價連續走升，週三中間價升至6.7249 創
三年八個月新高，惟市場靜待美國公布通膨數據，交投謹慎，人民幣僅小幅走升；
週三晚間，美國公布 9 月 CPI 年增率 3.7%、核心 CPI 年增 2.6%，雙雙高於預期，
帶動美元指數升至年內高點 102.945、 人民幣貶至周內低點6.7005； 然多位聯準會官
員發言表示聯準會不急於升息，令市場對於聯準會十月升息預期略有降溫，美元指
數自高點回落，激勵人民幣由貶轉升；臨近周末，聯準會主席華許於IMF 會議發言
並未指引十月聯準會政策利率走向，再度淡化市場對於聯準會十月升息預期，國際
美元走勢偏弱，人民幣走升觸及年內高點 6.6825，終場境外人民幣收在 6.6850，週
線升值 91 點，境內人民幣則收在 6.6870，週線升值 60 點。

＜未來分析＞ 
     預估本週人民幣震盪偏升。就國際市場來看，美國仍面臨高通膨、年底可能將
再次升息，利差因素支撐美元指數於高檔震盪，限縮人民幣升值空間；就中國國內
市場來看，中間價持續偏升將帶動人民幣走升，加以出口商實質拋匯需求將支撐人
民幣表現；最後就技術面來看，USD/CNH 日線圖中，匯價維持在五日均線之下交
投 ， 均線呈現空頭排列 ， 加以USD/CNH 有效跌破 6.6900 整數關卡 ， 顯示USD/CNH
空方勢力較盛，綜合以上，預估本週人民幣震盪偏升。 
＜預估本週＞ 美元兌人民幣：6.6500-6.7000

### Page 5

5  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌日圓匯率走勢  
         Oct/19/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
    上週 USD/JPY 先上後下。週初，受油價攀升推升美國通膨與利率預期帶動，美
元指數溫和走升創 6 月底以來新高，推升 USD/JPY 突破 158.50；週間，美國公布 9
月 CPI 高於預期，帶動美元指數升至 2025 年 5 月以來高點，也使 USD/JPY 最高來
到 158.94，然逢高獲利了結賣壓出籠使匯價回落；臨近週末，USD/JPY 一度下探至
157.53，隨後逢低買盤進場收復部分跌幅，終場收在 157.99，週線下跌 13 點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 USD/JPY 區間震盪，主要原因如下：一、受美伊戰事推升汽柴油飆漲
與服務成本擴散影響，美國 9 月 CPI 及 PPI 月增漲幅均超出市場預期，加上初請失
業金人數連五週低於 20 萬人顯示勞動市場穩定，市場預期聯準會將於 12 月再次升
息，美日實質利差維持高檔，持續為USD/JPY 提供下檔支撐。二、被視為鴿派的日
本經濟財政大臣城內實明確表態，貨幣政策應交由日本央行自行判斷，並宣告日本
已擺脫長期通縮、轉型為增長導向經濟體。此番言論降低政治干預日銀進一步升息
的疑慮，在貨幣政策正常化的腳步下，預計將限縮 USD/JPY 的上行空間。三、就技
術面而言，日線圖中，USD/JPY 匯價目前交投於月線及年線之間，各天期均線高度
糾結，多空雙方於 158 關卡附近拉鋸，在有效突破季線及年線反壓或跌破月線支撐
前 ， 仍以區間震盪看待。 綜合以上 ， 預估USD/JPY 本週呈現區間震盪的可能性較高 。 
＜預估本週＞ 美元兌日圓：157.00-159.00 
前週交易區間 
157.53 158.94

### Page 6

6  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
歐元兌美元匯率走勢  
         Oct/19/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
    上週 EUR/USD 震盪走升。週初，持續受法國財政赤字與歐債利差擔憂拖累，
EUR/USD 震盪走弱；週間，因美國9 月 CPI 高於預期，市場重新評估聯準會年底前
升息腳步，導致 EUR/USD 跳水最低來到 1.1112；臨近週末，儘管美國 PPI 與零售
銷售數據偏熱，市場優先反映美債殖利率回落與風險資產重新定價，美元多頭獲利
了結帶動 EUR/USD 谷底反彈 ， 最高來到1.1269， 終場收在1.1239， 週線上漲42 點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 EUR/USD 區間偏空震盪，主要原因如下：一、法國央行月度展望報
告指出，法國第三季 GDP 預估僅微幅成長 0.1%，印證近期高能源的成本已實質拖
累歐元區核心成員國增長。同時，歐洲央行總裁拉加德明確拒絕出手干預法債，要
求法國透過自身財政政策化解赤字困境；在明年法國大選前的財政隱憂與基本面疲
弱下，預計將令歐元上檔持續承壓。二、儘管歐洲央行管委溫施與總裁拉加德保留
12 月升息預期，加上德國基建與軍費支出為歐元提供部分下檔保護，但市場穩固預
期聯準會年底前將再升息一次。在美國經濟韌性與高利差優勢主導下，預計將限縮
歐元反彈空間。三、技術面而言，在日線圖中，EUR/USD 自低點反彈並站回五日線
之上，然而中長期均線下彎仍維持空頭排列，RSI 指標亦處於 50 中軸下方，顯示整
體空方趨勢尚未扭轉，反彈至月線附近恐面臨較大賣壓。綜合以上，預估EUR/USD
本週呈現區間偏空震盪的可能性較高。 
＜預估本週＞ 歐元兌美元：1.1150-1.1300 
前週交易區間 
1.1112 1.1269

7  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
                           新台幣利率走勢分析

115. 10.12-115.10.16 
銀行隔夜拆款利率 0.805% ~ 0.830%  
30 天期短票利率   1.67% ~ 1.68% 
公債－10 年期-A15109     2.020~2.025% 
－ 5 年期- A15108 1.88%

一、 台幣貨幣市場部分 ，本週央行連續減發定存單使市場資金寬鬆 ， 雖外資大舉匯
出，資金供給仍充裕，帶動短率朝區間低位移動。 
週五央行標售 2 年期定存單 250 億元 ， 加權平均得標利率為1.58%， 低於市場
預期區間 1.60%-1.62%，較上期標售結果下跌 5.8 個基本點。 
週 五 隔 夜 拆 款 利 率 區 間 在 0.805%~0.830% ， 30 天 期 短 票 主 要 成 交 在
1.67%~1.68%，利率大致持平於上週。 
二、 5 年期指標券 A15108 週二搓合成交在 1.88%，後隨美債利率滑落，加上本週
2 年期公債標債結果提振市場持券信心，帶動 10 年期指標券 A15109 週四向
下成交在 2.02%~2.025%。 
在銀行去化短期資金及補充 LCR 之需求下 ， 週三2 年期公債得標利率為1.65%，
落在市場預估區間下緣 ， 投標倍數回升至2.86 倍 ， 銀行業得標比重上升至88%。
股市承壓帶動避險需求，美債利率大幅回檔，促使台債交投偏空氛圍轉趨和
緩 ， 惟在央行維持緊縮貨幣政策下 ， 買盤積極追價的意願不強 ， 預期短線台債
殖利率呈區間格局的可能性較高，關注下週 10 年券標售結果，預估 5 年券區
間為 1.85%~1.90%，10 年券區間為 1.98%~2.03%。

## Document 098: 261012_ms_delta-USITC

### Page 1

M
Update
Delta Electronics Inc.  | Asia Pacific
Our Thoughts on USITC
Investigation No. 337
The near-term impact should be manageable, but we'll closely
monitor developments on this matter.
What's new:
• The US International Trade Commission (USITC) formally launched a patent
infringement investigation on October 9, 2026, following a complaint by
Vicor Corporation, a US-based power electronics company, against 20
entities, including Delta and others across the power supply, server assembly

and semiconductor space.
• Vicor alleges that certain imported vertical power delivery systems (used
mostly in HPC chips) have infringed US Patent 10,903,734.
• Vicor requested two types of remedy: 1) a Limited Exclusion Order (LEO);
and 2) Cease and Desist Orders (CDOs), which could restrict US imports and
sales of infringing products if granted.
Our view:
• We note that this is not the first time Delta and Vicor have had legal
disputes over patent infringement.

• We will closely monitor any developments in this matter, though we think
the impact on Delta's operations should be manageable (the products in
dispute account for high single digit % of its total revenue for 2026,  based
on our estimates).
• We remain OW.

Delta Electronics Inc. (2308.TW)
Base case: P/E multiple of 30x based on average 2027-28 EPS.                          We believe our target multiple
is justified by the 63% earnings CAGR we estimate for 2025-28, implying a PEG of 0.5, versus

ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

### Page 1

ab 12 October 2026
Global Research
Nanya Technology
Further upside to expectations likely
Another strong quarter
Nanya Tech (NTC) reported strong 3Q26 revenue of NT$133.6bn (+62% QoQ,
+612% YoY) in line with UBSe of NT$132.7bn and 6% above Visible Alpha consensus
of NT$126.5bn. EPS came in at $25.40, slightly below UBSe of NT$26.44 and modestly
above consensus of NT$24.40. Growth was driven primarily by strong ASPs, up 65%
QoQ (vs UBSe of +50%) mainly on the back of the renewal of 6-month and 1-year

contracts that had expired, partially offset by lower-than-expected bit growth of -
5% QoQ (vs UBSe of +5% QoQ). Profitability, again, improved sequentially, with gross/
operating margins rising to 85.7%/80.0% from 79.5%/73.7% in 2Q26.
Fulfilment rates to remain low until Fab 5A ramp
NTC continues to see supportive supply-demand dynamics, with fulfilment rates for
demand outside of long-term contracts – which account for close to 70% of bits already

– remaining low. That  said, NTC sees limited upside to unit shipments in the near term,
given low inventory levels and limited floor space: (1) reiterating its full-year bit growth
guidance of "up high-teens % YoY," implying 4Q unit shipments will decline MSD
% QoQ (UBSe: -6%); and (2) guiding shipments to remain flat thereafter until its new
fab begins to ramp in 2H27. NTC's efforts to bring Fab 5A online are progressing well,
with equipment move-in scheduled for 1Q27 and initial production expected by 3Q27.

NTC continues to expect wafer capacity to reach 30k wpm by the end of 2028 and 50k
by the end of 2030. With demand remaining strong and existing capacity not enough to
fully address this, NTC continues to see upside to sequential ASP growth in the near
term, although at a more moderate pace.
Changes to forecasts
Reflecting 3Q26 results, as well as our new bit growth and ASP forecasts, we keep our
2026E forecasts largely unchanged but slightly raise our 2027E/2028E revenue forecasts

by 7%/5% to NT$655bn/NT$735bn (from NT$613bn/NT$702bn previously) and
EPS forecasts by 10%/7% to NT$128.16/NT$134.53 (from NT$116.41/NT$125.68
previously). Our 2027E/2028E EPS forecasts now sit 10%/8% above pre-results Visible
Alpha consensus.
Valuation: PT NT$640/share; maintain Buy
We value Nanya Tech shares at 2.63x NTM P/BV based on our 2028E-31E ROE estimate
of 21.4% and CoE of 8.1%.

### Page 2

Nanya Technology UBS ResearchNanya Tech (2408.TW)
Thesis Map     UBS                    Research     THESIS MAP a guide to our thinking and what´s where in this report
Q: Will DRAM undersupply continue into 2028?
Yes, in our view. Agentic AI is boosting further memory demand on multiple fronts beyond HBM,
including DDR5/LPDDR5 for conventional servers and CPU head nodes in AI servers. We now forecast
an acceleration of memory demand growth into 2027 with DRAM bit end-consumption growing

40% YoY vs 21% in 2026. As almost all of the incremental wafer capacity addition for DRAM is
going to HBM, supply is not catching up with demand. The main risk remains affordability by
customers as memory industry revenues are expected to reach US$1.69tn in 2027E.
Q: Can NTC grow as a niche DRAM player?
Yes, we think 2027 is likely to be a year of more constrained growth. Subsequently, though, we think
its process tech migration to 1c nm and 1d nm will likely lead to a 30% increase in bits per wafer for

each process tech migration (at comparable yields). NTC is also slightly pulling in its new fab ramp,
with equipment expected to move in 1Q27. We also see a tail of DDR3 and DDR4 products NTC can
address for a while.
Nanya Tech shares trade at 2.17x NTM P/BV, above +1SD of its 10-year average. That said, we believe
the strength of the unfolding DRAM cycle, as well as the tail of legacy products that Nanya can
address as a niche player, should continue to provide support for the stock.

Our industry checks suggest HBM demand continues to result in capacity allocation away from DDR,
which may moderate possible cyclical corrections, supporting upside to DDR contract pricing for
longer.
At 2.17x NTM P/BV, we believe Nanya Tech shares are discounting a LT average ROE of 17.6% vs our
LT estimate of 21.4%.

Company Description
Nanya Tech is a pure DRAM company based in Taiwan, focusing on legacy products for
consumers, broad-based applications and is now growing in servers.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
Nanya Tech is a pure-play DRAM manufacturer based in Taiwan mainly focused on legacy DDR

products for customers across various end applications. We think downside risks to our thesis
could include a potential slowdown in end demand and hence the conventional memory
cycle as well as Nanya's legacy-centric product portfolio. We value Nanya Tech shares using a
target NTM P/BV multiple based on long-term average ROE and cost of equity.

horizon of 12 months.

document at  https://www.theocc.com/about/publications/character-risks.jsp  or ask your financial advisor for a copy.
Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.
Important Information About Sustainable Investing Strategies: Sustainable investing strategies aim to consider and incorporate environmental, social and governance
(ESG) factors into investment process and portfolio construction. Strategies across geographies approach ESG analysis and incorporate the findings in a variety of ways.
Incorporating ESG factors or sustainable investing considerations may inhibit the portfolio manager’s ability to participate in certain investment opportunities that otherwise

would be consistent with its investment objective and other principal investment strategies. The returns on a portfolio incorporating ESG factors or sustainable investing
considerations may be lower or higher than portfolios where ESG factors, exclusions, or other sustainability issues are not considered by the portfolio manager, and the
investment opportunities available to such portfolios may differ.

AVC (3017.TW): Liquid cooling rising penetration and AI servers ramp-up 
to drive growth ahead; Buy
19 October 2026 | 3:24AM HKT
3-month revenues preview: We expect AVC’s monthly revenues to continue 
sequential growth through Dec 2026, leading 4Q26 revenues to grow at +42% YoY/ 
+15% QoQ, driven by: (1) new AI server racks ramp-up, driving the overall liquid 
cooling penetration rate. As we highlighted in Oct (report link), we expect AI server 
rack (NVL72-equivalent) shipment (incl. Nvidia and AMD) to reach 63k/ 110k/ 164k 
in 2026-28E, reflecting our positive view on AI server racks. (2) Its major ASIC AI 
server customer’s new model ramp-up, driving its air-cooling and liquid-cooling 
components, along with a rising liquid cooling penetration rate. (3) Its customer base 
expansion to a new ASIC AI server, to further drive its liquid cooling components in 
4Q26. And (4) increasing adoption rate of ultra-thin VC in consumer electronics to fit 
the lightweight design and major customer’s foldable phone model. We expect AVC’s 
net income to grow at a +39% CAGR in 2026-28E, riding on the strong AI spending, a 
growing liquid cooling penetration rate, and AVC’s leading market position in total 
solutions (air cooling, liquid cooling with various components / systems). Maintain 
Buy.

### Page 2

Earnings revisions: We factor in AVC’s Sep revenue and revise down our 2026E net 
income by -1%, mainly on lower revenues in 4Q26E, reflecting the slow consumer 
electronics under high memory costs and AI servers model transition. We revise up our 
2027-28E net income by 4% / 0%, mainly on higher revenues and GMs. Our 2027-28E 
revenue is revised up by 1% / 1%, mainly on a higher contribution from AI server liquid 
cooling components. 2027-28E GM are revised up to reflect the rising liquid cooling 
components driving profitability.

Valuation: We continue to use a near-term P/E to derive our 12-month target price and 
update our target P/E multiple to 30.6x 2027E EPS (vs. 30.3x previously), which is still 
derived from PC/server peers’ EPS growth vs. P/E multiple correlation. Our target P/E 
multiple is above AVC’s historical average +1stv. forward trading P/E, reflecting our 
positive view on AVC riding on the rising penetration rate of liquid-cooled AI servers. 
With our updated target P/E and earnings estimates, we raise our 12-month target price 
to NT$4,736 (from NT$4,513). Maintain Buy.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - AVC 
We are Buy-rated on AVC. Our 12-month target price for AVC is based on a near-term 
P/E, consistent with our Taiwan Technology coverage. We base our target price of 
NT$4,736 on a target P/E multiple of 30.6x applied to our forward-year EPS (2027E). 
Our target P/E is derived from the correlation between P/E and EPS growth of its peers 
in technology hardware.  
Key downside risks: (1) slower-than-expected adoption of liquid cooling; (2) 
lower-than-expected demand for AI servers; (3) fiercer-than-expected competition; 
and (4) a slower-than-expected recovery in general servers.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

萬海 (2615 TT)  Buy

近洋線進入需求旺季，給予投資建議為 Buy。  
投資建議 ：考量 4Q26 上旬美國線運價相對有撐，近洋線進入需
求旺季，目前 PBR 評價處於近年區間中下緣，給予萬海投資建
議為 Buy ，目標價 140 元。  
近洋線 4Q26 進入需求旺季 ：受惠中國十一長假前的拉貨需求，
颱風因素導致港口作業效率降低，加上巴拿馬運河限制通行船
舶艘數、船舶周轉率下滑等因素影響之下，支撐美國線運價持
續走高；歐洲線運價走勢相對疲弱，主因需求逐漸進入旺季尾
聲，加上部分航線復航紅海、潛在運力增加所致。美國線方面，
10/2026 需求仍相對有撐，隨後將進入傳統需求淡季，而農曆春
節前拉貨需求有望再度回溫。巴拿馬運河先前受到降雨不足影
響而緊縮通航條件， 10/15/2026 開始每日通航預約名額放寬，
通行等候天數增加進而降低美東線船舶周轉率，通航條件改善
後，將進一步減弱對美國線運價的支撐力道。近洋線方面， 4Q26
進入傳統需求旺季，運價有望延續 3Q26 漲勢。   
預估 2026 年稅後 EPS 19.84 元： 1Q26 處於需求淡季，加上市
場預期紅海復航導致運價低迷，萬海 1Q26 營收 336.41 億元，
毛利率由 4Q25 的 23.92% 下滑至 22.76% ，營 業 利 益QoQ-7.86% ，
另業外認列金融資產評價利益，單季稅後獲利 76.72 億元，稅後
EPS 2.73 元。 2Q26 受惠於需求回升、運價調漲，營收
QoQ+27.52% ，毛利率 30.37% ，營業利益 QoQ+91.12% ；業外認
列金融資產評價利益之下 ， 單季稅後獲利 115.35 億元 ， 稅後EPS 
4.11 元。 3Q26 單季營收 QoQ+38.96% ，主因遠洋線為傳統需求
旺季，適逢氣候異常導致亞洲港口塞港、以及巴拿馬運河限航
等因素疊加影響下，支撐美國線運價持續走高，近洋線同樣呈
現上漲趨勢所致 。 預估單季稅後獲利 229.78 億元 ， 稅後EPS 8.19
元。 4Q26 亞洲線進入需求旺季，價量有望齊揚；遠洋線進入需
求淡季，美國線運價走勢將較 3Q26 修正，預估單季稅後獲利
134.88 億元，稅後 EPS 4.81 元。整體而言， 2026 年供過於求壓
力較 2025 年舒緩之下，預估萬海 2026 年稅後獲利 556.72 億
元， YoY+76.94% ，稅後 EPS 19.84 元。

### Page 2

個股報告  
                                        2026 年 10 月 13 日

2 群益投顧 
  公司 ESG 政策：  
(一 )企業對於因應氣候變遷或溫室氣體管理之策略  
1.萬海秉持對環境保護的承諾，除符合 IMO 國際公約規範之下，進行船隊
減排，減少空汙，並且落實  設備保養及改善，確保機器良好運作，以達達
到環保之要求，愛護地球，永續經營。  2. 萬海積極參與國際環保平台，透
過實際行動來推行節能減碳的環保策略：  (1)2015 年參與 Clean Cargo 
Working Group (CCWG) 組織，加強與同業分享環保知識、資訊、技  術的交
流，以推動節能減碳為目的。自 2017 年起依據 CCWG 新規範，萬海提供
CCWG 的船隊相關航  行、油耗數據須由驗證單位檢驗，萬海船隊已連續五
年 (2016 ~ 2021 年度 )排放數據均通過第三方  驗證單位 DNV 之驗證，此將
可主動吸引同為其會員的貨主、承攬業選擇使用符合其環保政策之航商。
(2) 持續參與世界航運協會 (World Shipping Council,WSC) ，針對各項航運事
務及環境保護議題，  進行討論。萬海為該協會會員，副董事長為該協會之
理事會成員。  (3)2019 年加入零排放聯盟 (Getting to ZeroCoalition) ，萬海
持續推動並實現國際航運減少碳  排放為宗旨，致力達成該聯盟目標。  
(二 )企業溫室氣體排放量減量目標  
依照 IMO 規定，以 2008 年為基準降低碳排率，  以 2021 年為止減少 30%
碳排放率，並預計於 2030 年可  以達到 50% 減排率目標，優於 IMO 要求；
未來將持續進行船隊汰舊換新計畫，並配以新型節能主機及  各式最新節能
設備，以達節能減碳、環境保護目標。  
(三 )企業溫室氣體排放量減量之預算與計畫  
1.預算：保護地球本為每一個企業之責任，本公司將以提升船舶性能，降低
船隊油耗，使用低硫燃  油，以降低溫室氣體排放，視該成本為必要性支出。
2. 船舶能源效率管理計畫 (Ship Energy Efficiency Management Plan
(SEEMP) ：  依國際海事組織 (IMO) 之船舶能源效率管理計畫 (Ship Energy 
Efficiency ManagementPlan, SEEMP) 規範訂定有效的能源效率管理制度，並
根據能源效率設計指標 (EnergyEfficient Design Index, EEDI) 作為新船能源
效率的標準，並據實揭露能源效率營運指標 (Energy Efficient Operational
Index, EEOI) 。  3. 萬海另依 IMO 法規要求制定 SEEMP PART II ，加入「燃
油消耗數據收集計畫」 (DataCollection Plan, DCP) ，藉由執行燃油消耗數據
的監測，管理和報告船舶於海上航行時所產生的二氧化碳排  放，並依法規
製作年度排放報告，經第三方查核認證後提交給國際海事組織。  
4Q26 近洋線進入需求旺季：  
3Q26 以來遠洋線走勢呈現分化。受惠中國十一長假前的拉貨需求，颱風因
素導致港口作業效率降低，加上巴拿馬運河限制通行船舶艘數、船舶周轉率
下滑等因素疊加影響之下，支撐美國線運價持續走高；歐洲線運價走勢相對
疲弱，主因需求逐漸進入旺季尾聲，加上部分航線復航紅海、潛在運力增加
所致。  
美國線方面， 10/2026 需求仍相對有撐，隨後將進入傳統需求淡季，而農曆
春節前拉貨需求有望再度回溫。巴拿馬運河先前受到降雨不足影響而緊縮
通航條件， 10/15/2026 開始每日通航預約名額放寬至 33 艘 (正常為 36 艘 )，

資料來源：群益投顧彙整  
燃油價格處於高檔震盪：  
油價方面，近期新加坡低硫油價格持續回落。美伊戰爭前約 500 美元 /噸，
戰爭後一度上漲至 1,120 美元 /噸，近期則來到近 890 美元 /噸。以目前運價
水準而言，已完全轉嫁燃油價格變動對營運成本造成的影響。  
新船下水規劃：  
公司至 2025 年底船隊達 116 艘，運力共計 57.27 萬 TEU。 2026 年預計交付
6 艘船舶，包括 2 艘 7,000TEU 及 4 艘 8,700TEU 船舶， 2027 年預計交付 1
艘 8,700TEU 及 10 艘 16,000TEU 船舶。  
營收比重方面， 2025 年美國線比重約 23% 、中東印度線比重約 27% 、亞洲
近洋線比重約 37% 、南美線比重約 13% 。  
預估 2026 年稅後 EPS 19.84 元 : 
1Q26 處於需求淡季，加上市場預期紅海復航導致運價低迷，萬海 1Q26 營
收 336.41 億元，毛利率由 4Q25 的 23.92% 下滑至 22.76% ，營業利益 QoQ-
7.86% ，另業外認列金融資產評價利益，單季稅後獲利 76.72 億元，稅後 EPS
2.73 元。  
2Q26 受惠於需求回升、運價調漲，營收 QoQ+27.52% ，毛利率 30.37% ，營
業利益 QoQ+91.12% ；業外認列金融資產評價利益之下，單季稅後獲利
115.35 億元，稅後 EPS 4.11 元。  
3Q26 單季營收 QoQ+38.96% ，主因遠洋線為傳統需求旺季，適逢氣候異常
導致亞洲港口塞港、以及巴拿馬運河限航等因素疊加影響下，支撐美國線運
價持續走高，近洋線同樣呈現上漲趨勢所致。預估單季稅後獲利 229.78 億
元，稅後 EPS 8.19 元。

7 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌，亦將出具降評報告。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

HiMSEN 엔진 및 SMR 주기기 생산설비 대규모 증설 발표 
- 9/10 HD현대중공업은 총 1조 722억원 규모의 시설투자 2건을 공시 
① 대출력 발전엔진(4행정 중속엔진, HiMSEN엔진) 생산능력 확충에 8,336
억원 투입, 글로벌 전력수요 증가에 따른 엔진발전 시장 확대에 대응하는 목
적. 대출력 발전엔진 전용 공장 신설과 토지 취득, 건축물·기계설비 등 생산설
비 구축이 골자. 투자기간은 ‘26.09~’28.05(설계/인허가 ~’27.02, 공장건설 
~’28.05. ’28.06 상업운전개시, 28년 가동률 30%, 2030년 100% 도달) 
② SMR 주기기 제작 생산시설 구축에 2,386억원(자기자본 대비 2.55%) 투
입, SMR 주기기 제작사업 진입 및 수주 물량 증가에 대응. SMR 전용 공장 
및 설비 신설과 기존 공장 최적화를 병행, 투자기간은 ’26.10~’29.04. 
기다리던 증설 공시. 늘어난 Capa로 AI DC향 수혜 확대 기대 
- 25년 3.0GW(선박용 2.3GW + 육상용 0.7GW)였던 HiMSEN 엔진 생산 
Capa는 2030년 7.2GW(선박용 3.2GW + 육상용 4.0GW)로 확대. 그간 선
박용 중심이었던 엔진 사업의 포트폴리오를 발전용으로 본격 다각화하는 사
업 재편으로 해석 
- AI DC 신규 증설 확대 추세 속 계통 연결의 어려움과 CCGT, Aero -
derivative 대비 납기 및 부하상승시간, 모듈화 용이성 영역에서 장점을 가진 
발전용 중속엔진에 대한 수요 지속 기대(260908 당사 보고서 참조) 
- 지난 Corban Energy Group 향 계약 건(1,000MW, $ 674 mn)을 단순 
적용 시 금번 증설의 향후 매출액 기여규모는 연간 $ 2.8 bn에 달함. 엔진 시
장의 Seller-Market 기조에 따라 20%를 상회하는 높은 이익률이 지속될 것
으로 전망함에 따라 향후 유의미한 이익 성장 기대 
- HD현대마린솔루션 또한 LTSA 수주 저변 확대 측면에서 향후 이익 확장 가
능. HiMSEN엔진 고유 라이선스 보유에 따른 시너지로 그룹사 전반의 수혜

## Document 231: AIと金融市場、強まる相互依存 ～IMFの国際金融安定性報告書から読み解く新たなリスク～

Source PDF: all report pdf/AIと金融市場、強まる相互依存 ～IMFの国際金融安定性報告書から読み解く新たなリスク～.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-10-15
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Flash Insights

2026 年10 月 15 日

1/5 
AI と金融市場、強まる相互依存 
～IMF の国際金融安定性報告書から読み解く新たなリスク～ 
 資産運用調査部 主席研究員  奥田 宏二

1. はじめに 
国際通貨基金（IMF）は10 月 13 日、国際金融安定性報告書（Global Financial Stability 
Report、GFSR）の全体版を公表した。金融市場は底堅さを維持していると評価したうえで、金融
安定上のリスクは高いとした。主なリスクとして①人工知能（AI）関連資産の大幅な値下がり、
②国債利回りの急上昇、③サイバー攻撃の三つを取り上げた。 
GFSRは世界経済の動向を分析する「世界経済見通し」 （WEO）と並ぶIMFの主要な定期報告書だ。
株式・債券市場や銀行、ノンバンクなどを幅広く点検し、金融システムの安定を損なうリスクや
ショックを増幅させる脆弱性を分析する。各国の金融当局や市場関係者にとって、その時々の金
融市場・システムの課題を示すものとして注目度が高い。本稿では、GFSRが今回示した三つのリ
スクを整理する（資料1）。特にAIが金融市場・システムにもたらす影響を詳しくみていく。

資料 1 GFSR（2026 年 10 月版）があげた三つのリスク

（出所）IMF「GFSR（2026 年 10 月）」より第一ライフ資産運用経済研究所作成 
リスク 評価の変化 評価の概要
AI関連資産
の大幅な値
下がり
横ばい
引き金：AI関連投資の規模や持続性、収益性が期待を下回る。
評価：AI関連資産の規模が拡大し ているため、影響は大きくな
り得る。中核的なAI関連企業の収益は好調で、株価の割高感は
和らいだ一方、AI関連分野でレバレッジが拡大し ている。
国債利回り
の急上昇
悪化
引き金：インフレ再燃、財政問題でタームプレミア ムが上昇。
評価：国債市場は金融市場の基盤で影響は深刻になり得る。高
水準の政府債務や借り換えリスクは未解決のため、財政・イン
フレのショックをより頻繁に受けやすい。
サイバー攻
撃
悪化
引き金：重要な金融機関や市場インフラがサイバー攻撃で被害
評価：デジタル化の急速な進展で攻撃対象が広がり、影響は深
刻になり得る。最先端のAIモデルは攻撃と防御の双方に利用さ
れる可能性。

### Page 2

2/5 
2. AI関連資産の大幅な値下がりリスク  
一つ目のリスクにあげたのはAI関連資産の大幅な値下がりだ。4 月版からの変化を「横ばい」と
評価した。しかし、これは状況が変わっていないという意味ではない。AI関連の中核企業が高い
利益成長を続け、株価の割高感が薄れた点を評価した一方、巨額の設備投資によるフリーキャッ
シュフローの減少や社債発行の増加、投資家によるレバレッジ拡大を警戒している。 
IMFは、AI関連投資を世界経済や金融市場の重要な下支え要因と評価している。生成AIなどの普
及に伴い、データセンターや半導体投資は活発で、関連企業の業績は好調だ。AI関連企業の株価
が急上昇した一方、予想利益も大きく伸びている。GFSRは、一部の株式市場で予想利益の伸びが
株価上昇を上回り、予想株価収益率（PER）が低下した点を評価している。 
一方、リスクが高まっている点として注目するのは、大規模なクラウドサービスやデータセン
ターを運営する企業（ハイパースケーラー）のフリーキャッシュフロー（事業で稼いだ現金から
設備投資などを差し引いた額）の減少だ。設備投資の急増により、資金調達のため社債発行が拡
大している。IMFによると、世界の投資適格社債発行額に占めるハイパースケーラーの割合は、
2024 年の1％未満から2026 年には約10％に上昇した（資料2）。社債の信用スプレッドも、4 月版
GFSRの公表以降、投資適格社債市場全体と比べて約30 ベーシスポイント拡大したという（注 1）。

資料 2 世界の投資適格社債発行額に占めるハイパースケーラーの割合

（出所）IMF「GFSR（2026 年 10 月）」より第一ライフ資産運用経済研究所作成

現在のAI関連企業の資金調達の特徴として、資金と売上が企業間で相互に結びつく循環的な取
引があげられる。具体的には、大手企業が財務基盤の相対的に弱いAI企業に出資し、出資先がそ

### Page 3

3/5 
の資金で大手企業のサービスや半導体を購入する仕組みだ。GFSRによれば、2023 年以降に公表さ
れた 14 件の取引では、株式投資が約3,000 億ドル、関連する債務が約6,500 億ドルにのぼり、将
来の売上に関する契約は約1 兆 4,000 億ドルに達する。こうした取引はうまくいけば、出資先の
企業価値拡大による投資リターンの増加と売上増加が期待できる。一方、投資先の経営が悪化す
れば、投資損失と将来の売上減少という逆回転が生じる。IMFは、こうした取引による投資資金や
将来の売上が少数の企業に集中しており、特定の投資先の経営悪化がAI関連企業の間で損失を広
げるリスクを指摘している。 
AI関連企業に投資する側の懸念としてGFSRが取り上げたのは、株価上昇が続くなか、借り入れ
などを利用した取引が拡大し、投資家が過大なリスクを抱えている可能性だ。ヘッジファンドの
株式ポジションと信用取引融資残高の合計は、2024 年末の先進国GDP比約10％から2026 年には約
15％に増加した。また、レバレッジ型上場投資信託（ETF）に占める個別株を対象としたETFの割
合は、16％から28％に上昇した。GFSRの補足分析によると、株式オプションの価格から推計した
レバレッジ取引の資金調達コストも、2026 年初めからほぼ倍増している（注 2）。レバレッジをか
けた取引は株価が下落した際の損失が大きくなり、追加証拠金を拠出するために他の資産を売ら
ざるを得なくなるといった形で、他の金融商品にも波及する場合がある。

3. 国債利回りの急上昇リスク  
AIと同様に金融市場で注目が高まっている国債について、GFSRは利回りの急上昇リスクを4 月
版から「悪化」と評価した。特に注目したいのは、長期国債を保有するリスクに応じて投資家が
求める上乗せ金利（タームプレミアム）の上昇だ。IMFによると、10 年国債利回りの上昇には将来
の政策金利の予想とタームプレミアムの双方が寄与しており、特に先進国のタームプレミアムは
歴史的な変動範囲の上限に近い水準にある。 
タームプレミアムを押し上げているのは、財政赤字の継続や高水準の政府債務に対する懸念、
今後の国債発行増加の見通しだ。ユーロ圏、英国、米国では、2026 年1～3 月期に投資適格社債の
発行が国債を含む債券発行総額の4 割を占めた（デュレーション加重） 。さらにGFSRは、AI関連企
業による社債発行が急増し、国債と投資家の資金を奪い合うことで、国債市場への追加的な圧力
になっているとの指摘も紹介している。 
IMFが警戒するのは、インフレ再燃などをきっかけにタームプレミアムがさらに上昇する事態
だ。主要国では、政府債務が歴史的に高い水準にあるという問題が未解決のままで、国債の借り
換えリスクも高い。特に、国債の償還年限を短期化した国では、金利が上昇すると高い金利で借
り換える必要が生じ、財政負担が増える。長期債から短期債への置き換えが大規模に進めば、こ
うしたリスクは一段と高まる可能性がある。 
政府債務が増えて国債発行が膨らむと、国内の銀行や保険会社などだけでは増発分を消化しき
れず、外国人投資家や投資ファンドの存在感が高まりやすい。こうした投資家は価格変動に敏感
で、インフレ懸念が強まった際に長期国債の売却が集中する可能性がある。GFSRでは、これをタ
ームプレミアムの上昇を増幅する要因としてあげている。

### Page 4

4/5 
GFSRは、日本国債市場の流動性悪化を指摘している。日本銀行によるイールドカーブ・コント
ロールの終了後、金融政策の正常化が進むなかで利回りの変動が大きくなったことに加え、特に
長期国債で売値と買値の差が拡大している点をあげた。ユーロ圏と英国では流動性を示す指標が
改善し、米国でも足元は良好な状況にあるのとは対照的だ。 
IMFはタームプレミアムが急上昇した場合、AI関連株を含む幅広い資産の価格が下落するほか、
金融取引に利用される国債の担保価値も低下し、金融市場全体に混乱が広がる可能性を警戒して
いる。IMFは世界各国の銀行を対象に、国債利回りの急上昇やAIの生産性向上効果が期待を下回る
ことなどを想定したストレステストを実施した。この結果、世界全体の銀行システムの自己資本
比率は規制上の最低水準を上回るものの、ストレス後の普通株式等Tier1 比率が 7％を下回る銀行
の資産は、世界の銀行資産全体の22％を占める結果になった（注3）。

4. サイバー攻撃のリスク 
GFSRは、三つ目のリスクとしてあげたサイバーリスクについて、「悪化」したと評価した。GFSR
では金融機関が外部の情報技術サービスへの依存を強めている点を問題視した。クラウドサービ
スやソフトウエアを提供する企業の一部に利用が集中し、多くの金融機関が共通の技術基盤を利
用するようになっている。こうした企業が攻撃を受けてサービスを停止すれば、金融機関への影
響が広範に及ぶ可能性がある。さらに、外部委託先のつながりが複雑になり、リスクの全体像を
把握しにくくなっている点も指摘した。 
サイバー攻撃は高度化が進み、日本でも企業の情報流出が相次ぐ。GFSRは、最先端のAIモデル
がネットワーク攻撃の模擬実験で高い能力を示していると指摘した。GFSRによると、悪用が確認
されたソフトウエアなどの脆弱性のうち、公表日以前または公表当日に悪用された割合（ゼロデ
イ比率）は、2026 年の年初来で70％を超え、前年から約20 ポイント上昇した。脆弱性が公表さ
れてから悪用されるまでの時間も短くなっており、ソフトウエアの提供企業が修正プログラムを
用意する余裕は小さくなっている。

5. おわりに 
今回のGFSRからは、AIの急成長に金融市場や金融システムがどこまで対応できるのかという問
題意識が垣間見える。AI関連企業の利益成長が世界的な株高をけん引する一方、巨額の設備投資
に必要な資金を調達するため、社債市場でもその存在感を高め始めた。技術革新が進むほど投資
資金への需要も膨らみ、あらゆる市場から資金を吸い上げる構図だ。 
成長分野に資金が集中し、市場が過熱することは過去にも繰り返されてきた。今回のAIブーム
で特徴的なのは、巨額の資金需要が国債市場の需給にまで影響を及ぼす可能性があることだ。AI
の成長が金融市場を動かす一方、金利の上昇が今度はAI投資の採算を左右する。相互依存の強ま
りが今回のGFSRからも確認できたといえる。 
以 上

### Page 5

経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。
5/5
【注釈】
1) IMF「Global Financial Stability Report」（2026 年 10 月版） 、第1 章補足分析1.1「Rising
Leverage in the AI Ecosystem」3 頁。
2) 上記補足分析1.1、5～6 頁、図 1.1.4。資金調達コストは、S&P500 の株式オプション価格から
推計した担保付翌日物調達金利に対する30 日間の上乗せ金利。
3) IMF「Global Financial Stability Report」（2026 年 10 月版） 、第1 章 7～9 頁、図1.7 および
補足分析1.2「2026 IMF Global Banking Stress Test」。ストレステストの対象は29 カ国の

665 銀行。

## Document 272: Cliff Notes_ mulling the minutes (2026-10-22)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Cliff Notes_ mulling the minutes (2026-10-22).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-10-22
Pages: 11
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

#cliffnotes #rba #australia #inflation #us #fomc #ecb #aud #usd
Cliff Notes: mulling the minutes
By Sian Fenner, Ryan Wells & Illiana Jain
11:10 October 09 2026 Share
Key insights from the week that was.
The only data release of note in Australia this week was the October Westpac-MI Consumer Sentiment
Survey, which reported a 4.7% drop in the headline index. At 80.4, sentiment has tumbled back to
deeply pessimistic levels, similar to the post-pandemic cost-of-living shock when readings were among

the weakest in the survey’s more than fifty-year history. Indeed, inflation and interest rate pressures are
once again clearly front of mind for consumers, with both the view on ‘family finances vs a year ago’

### Page 2

and ‘family finance next 12 months’ falling 8.0% and 6.4% in the month, respectively. Moreover, the
RBA’s September rate hike looks to have had a significant impact – sentiment tanked 20% between
those surveyed before the RBA decision and those surveyed afterwards.

Views on the economic outlook were relatively more resilient, but they still weakened, with the one-year
and five-year ahead sub-indexes falling 1.6% and 0.4% respectively. Consumers are becoming more
‘on edge’ about job security, albeit not outright alarmed. The unemployment rate remains relatively low
versus history, although it has drifted up to a cycle high of 4.6% as labour supply continues to outstrip
demand. We expect it to hold there in next week’s September Labour Force Survey – see our preview
for more detail. All told, with higher interest rates, renewed inflation pressures and a softening jobs
market all weighing at once, households are entering the final months of the year on the back foot.

Offshore, the focus was on US data.

Before turning to this week's releases, it is worth noting that September non-farm payrolls came in
softer than expected across most metrics. Employment rose by only 29k (89k expected), and there
were sizeable downward revisions to prior months, with a combined 60k removed from July and August
payrolls, leaving gains of just 31k and 29k respectively. The details pointed to weaker hiring in leisure &
hospitality and education & health services industries, although construction and manufacturing
employment continued to benefit from ongoing data-centre-related investment. The unemployment rate
edged up to 4.2%, with softer employment occurring alongside a rise in labour force participation to
61.8%. Meanwhile wage growth moderated to 0.1%mth (3.0%yr). Taken together, the report suggests
labour demand is cooling, with the three-month average pace of job creation slowing to around 50k.
However, softer payroll gains are not necessarily indicative of labour market deterioration. Lower
immigration and ageing demographics have reduced the pace of employment growth needed to keep
the unemployment rate stable. As such, the report is more consistent with a labour market that is
gradually normalising than one entering a meaningful downturn.

The September FOMC minutes reinforced a distinctly hawkish message, with participants noting they
had "not seen sufficient progress on lowering inflation in recent months" and expressing concern that
inflation risks remained "skewed to the upside". Policymakers highlighted that both core services and
core goods inflation remained elevated, with some attributing persistent goods price strength to the
effects of the AI buildout despite the sector's traditional disinflationary role. There was also concern
that, after more than five years of inflation above target, elevated inflation could begin to influence
inflation expectations and wage- and price-setting behaviour. In contrast, labour market conditions
were generally viewed as stable and "close to maximum employment". While a majority noted the
labour market had strengthened somewhat recently, several participants highlighted unusually low
dynamism, characterised by low hiring, low layoffs, a low job-finding rate and elevated long-term
unemployment. Importantly, participants generally judged that aggregate wage growth was moderate
and "consistent with inflation moving toward 2 percent", with several explicitly noting that "the labor
market was not currently a source of inflationary pressures".

### Page 3

Related articles
A key focus was the interaction between AI investment, productive capacity and inflation. While
participants broadly expected AI-related investment to boost productivity growth and potential output
over time, several warned that "the AI buildout could cause aggregate demand to outpace aggregate
supply over the medium term, putting upward pressure on inflation". Consistent with this view, some
participants had raised their estimate of the neutral policy rate and several judged the current policy
stance to be "not restrictive or only mildly restrictive". Against this backdrop, all participants supported
the September rate increase, and most assessed that another increase in the federal funds rate would
likely be appropriate by year-end, although future decisions would remain data dependent.

The September ISM Services PMI released this week painted a similar picture of an economy that
remains resilient but is gradually losing momentum. The headline index eased to 54.9 from 55.4 in
August, with the decline driven by a sharp fall in business activity and a 9.4pt drop in new export
orders, which slipped into contractionary territory for the first time in eight months. Labour market
conditions improved modestly with the employment index moving to broadly neutral territory 50.1 after
two months below 50, supported by elevated backlogs and still-solid demand. Respondent
commentary illustrated the mixed nature of labour demand, with some firms reporting they were "filling
positions vacated due to promotions or retirements", while others cited "restructuring due to efficiencies
gained using AI tools". Cost pressures also remained elevated, with the prices paid index rising to 74.0,
its highest level since July 2022 and around 18pts above its historical average, while fuel costs, tariffs
and supply chain constraints remained the most frequently cited concerns. Overall, the report suggests
services activity remains consistent with ongoing expansion, albeit at a slower pace, while labour
demand and pricing pressures remain firm.

Overall, the data supports our view that the FOMC will move to a broadly neutral policy stance by the
end of the year with one more rate hike, although the case for a back-to-back move in October has
become less clear cut.

持 1450 亿美元不变。

⼀股将获得⼀股新股，这实质上等同于⼀次 “ ⼀拆⼆ ” 拆股。
Meta 发⾏⽆投票权新股，换取扎克伯格承诺留居加州
Meta 与扎克伯格达成协议，将向其发⾏⽆投票权 C 类股票。作为交换，扎克伯格承诺未来⼋年
继续居住在加州或 Meta 总部所在的其他司法辖区。

这项安排也与加州拟议中的亿万富豪财富税有关。扎克伯格曾表⽰，相关税收可能迫使其出售
具有投票权的 B 类股票以筹集税款，使其在公司的投票权⽐例降⾄ 53% 以下。
根据协议，扎克伯格可以⽴即出售所获 C 类股票的 20% ，相当于其整体经济权益的 10% ，其余股
份的出售限制将在⼋年内逐步解除。
M e t a 特 别 委 员 会 称 ， 确 保 扎 克 伯 格 在 公 司 总 部 办 公 ⾄ 关 重 要 ， 尤 其 是 在 公 司 与 M e t a
Superintelligence Labs 密切合作、 AI 竞争⽇益激烈之际。
Meta ⾸席财务官 Susan Li 则表⽰， C 类股票主要作为融资⼯具，帮助公司更灵活地为 AI 业务筹
资，同时避免稀释现有股东的投票权。
⼴告业务保持增⻓，第四季度营收指引超预期
⼴告业务仍是 Meta 业绩增⻓的主要⽀撑。第三季度，公司⼴告展⽰量同⽐增⻓ 10% ，平均⼴告
价格上涨 14% 。家庭⽇活跃⼈数达到 36.4 亿，同⽐增⻓ 3% 。

Meta 预计第四季度营收为 730 亿⾄ 760 亿美元，区间中值为 745 亿美元，较上年同期增⻓约
24% 。该指引⾼于 LSEG 汇总的分析师平均预期 730 亿美元左右，显⽰公司对年末⼴告需求和业
务增⻓保持信⼼。
⼴告业务的增⻓也为 AI 投资提供了重要⽀撑。 Meta 正将 AI 应⽤于⼴告投放和定向等环节，同时
尝试通过新的 AI 产品拓展⼴告以外的收⼊来源。不过，新业务的商业化进展能否匹配基础设施投
⼊，仍有待后续业绩验证。
AI 助⼿ Muse 成商业化核⼼，但障碍犹存
Meta 将消费者 AI 助⼿ Muse 定位为整体战略的核⼼，并围绕负责该产品的 Meta 超级智能实验室对
公司架构进⾏重组，同时宣布推出⾯向企业客⼾销售 AI ⼯具的新业务线。
据市场情报机构 SensorTower 数据， Muse ⾃ 9 ⽉上线以来下载量已超过 1000 万次，该应⽤提供
每⽉ 20 美元和 100 美元两档订阅⽅案。

中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

⽤⼾使⽤ Facebook 和 Instagram 的⽅式作出⼀系列限制，指控称 Meta 平台被刻意设计以使⼉童
成瘾。

就其与第三⽅共享⽤⼾数据的⽅式及平台仇恨⾔论和虚假信息政策执⾏情况存在误导⾏为，该
案涉及英国政治咨询公司剑桥分析。
不过，费⽤压⼒并不完全来⾃单⼀法律事项。 Meta 第三季度总成本与费⽤同⽐增⻓ 68% ，达到
516.1 亿美元，明显快于 26% 的营收增幅。营业利润率由上年同期的 40% 降⾄ 20% ，反映出公司
在扩⼤ AI 投⼊的同时，盈利能⼒受到挤压。
Meta 预计 2026 年全年总费⽤将达到 1750 亿⾄ 1790 亿美元。随着 AI 相关⽀出继续增⻓，投资者需
要同时衡量⼴告业务的增⻓、成本扩张速度以及现⾦流变化，⽽不能仅凭剔除⼀次性费⽤后的

每股收益判断经营表现。

Charts of the week: No capitulation

A hotter-than-expected US inflation report triggered a pullback in risk assets, but there are few signs of capitulation so far 
US core and supercore inflation; US inflation breadth*    Our ‘Fear and Greed’ indicator

Source: Bloomberg, Standard Chartered; *share of US consumer inflation components rising more than 3%

Editorial 
Staying defensive and diversified  
Strategy summary:  Two weeks ago, we turned defensive 
towards equities, locking in semiconductor equity gains amid 
elevated bond volatility, narrowing market breadth and political 
uncertainty before the US mid -term elections. The near -term 
outlook reflected a less favourable risk-reward balance. Since 
we turned defensive , the Nasdaq 100 index has recorded its 
biggest weekly decline in three months as September’s hotter-
than-expected US inflation report briefly lifted bond yields. 
We have extended our defensive stance this week, 
downgrading US Communication Services equities to Neutral 
and upgrading US Healthcare to Overweight. We also closed 
our opportunistic bullish Communication Services idea and 
initiated a bullish idea on Healthcare, where earnings visibility 
and defensive characteristics appear more attractive. In fixed 
income, we see opportunities in US 10-year government bonds. 
We await clearer AI profit margins and spending guidance, 
broader earnings growth or lower bond and equity market 
volatility before turning positive on equities. Until then, 
diversification and disciplined entry levels remain important. 
Hot US inflation triggers equity pullback; not hot enough 
for an October Fed hike:  Risk assets pulled back after 
September’s hotter-than-expected inflation data (headline 3.7% 
y/y; core 2.6% y/y). Higher gasoline and diesel prices were the 
main drivers, but passthrough appeared in airline fares, used 
cars and transportation services, showing pressures were not 
confined to energy. Almost half of inflation components are still 
rising above 3%, a measure recently cited by Fed Chair Warsh. 
Producer inflation also beat estimates. The reports revive the 
possibility of an October Fed rate hike, but we expect a divided 
Fed to weigh it against September’s soft jobs report and 
narrowly hold this month. A December hike appears more likely 
if inflation remains firm . Although Fed Chair Warsh did not 
comment on policy at an IMF speech this week, New York Fed 
chief Williams said he saw no need to hike in 2027. Our base 
case remains two further hikes by June 2027. 
Limited equity pullback so far.  Equities fell sharply after the 
US inflation report, but the pullback is limited. The Nasdaq 100 
is 4.5% below its record high, while the S&P 500 remains close 
to all -time highs despite the latest decline. The S&P 500 
volatility index (VIX) has risen from below 15 to 1 8.2, still well 
under the 25 –30 range typically associated with capitulation 
and forced selling. Meanwhile, US government bond volatility 
(MOVE) remains elevated after surging since September.  Our 
stock-bond quant model turned more negativ e (-2) this week, 
signalling elevated equity volatility in the near term.  
When to turn more constructive on stocks?  We would turn 
more constructive when capitulation signals align: the Nasdaq 
100 approaches a 10% drawdown, the VIX rises to 25 –30 and 
our ‘Fear and Greed’ index  enters “extreme fear”. Greater 
confidence would also require easing concerns about AI 
spending and technology firms’ exposure to neocloud revenue, 
lower bond volatility and our stock-bond model turning positive 
again. Until these conditions emerge, volatility may remain 
elevated, favouring a defensive equity stance. Political risk 
should ease after the US mid -term elections. We continue to 
seek selective opportunities following excessive corrections. 
US financials remain a preferred sector after Q3 earnings:  
US large banks’ earnings were mixed, but underlying trends 
remain encouraging. Healthy loan and deposit growth supports 
net interest income, while resilient consumer spending, wealth 
management activity and a solid M&A and refinancing pipeline 
strengthen the outlook. Credit risks appear manageable . We 
remain constructive and favour stronger, diversified franchises. 
US government bonds offer value as yields spike:  The 
reversal in US government bond yields after their initial inflation-
driven spike suggests limited further upside. The 10 -year yield 
briefly reached 5.32% before slumping as falling equities 
prompted short-covering. We expect a near -term 5.00–5.25% 
range and view moves above 5.25% as attractive entry points 
for our opportunistic 10-year government bond idea. Structural 
pressures, including term premium and increasing supply to 
fund fiscal deficits and AI investments, prevent us from turning 
outright bullish on longer maturities. We, therefore, continue to 
prefer 3–7-year maturities in broader bond allocations.  
—  Rajat Bhattacharya and Sundeep Gantori

### Page 4

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 16 October 2026

Top client questions 
  How does the September 2026 US Consumer Price Index 
(CPI) report alter your view on the USD and bond yields?   
Our view: The hotter-than-expected CPI print raises the odds of 
an October Fed hike, though we still expect a hold followed by 
a rate hike in December. In the near term, we maintain our view 
that the US 10 -year government bond yield will range between 
5.00% and 5.25%, while  the USD will remain supported as 
markets reassess the likelihood of further Fed hikes. Additional 
gains would depend partly on markets pricing a faster or more 
prolonged tightening cycle, while clearer signs of an 
approaching policy rate peak could see the USD ease back. 
Rationale: September US consumer inflation  came in above 
consensus across the board – headline CPI rose 3.7% y/y (vs. 3.6% 
estimate), and core CPI rose 2.6% y/y (vs. 2.5% estimate) – marking 
the fastest headline acceleration in five months. This shifts the 
balance of risks modestly hawkish . Unsurprisingly, the market -
implied probability of an October Fed hike rose over 50% overnight 
but closed the day at 32.7% – 9ppt higher than Tuesday’s close. 
We believe a split Fed will weigh this inflation report against the soft 
September jobs report and narrowly vote to keep rates on hold this 
month. Our base case remains for two more Fed hikes  by June 
2027. A December rate hike  now looks more like a done deal, 
especially as persistently higher oil prices keep headline and core 
inflation elevated for at least the next few months. Any re-escalation 
of the Middle East conflict after the  US mid-term elections would 
exert pressure on the Fed, although we believe Republicans would  
show greater restraint in US foreign action if they suffer steep Senate 
losses, which looks increasingly likely. 
The intra-day price action in US government bonds was instructive. 
The US 10-year yield briefly spiked to 5.32% on the print before 
closing materially lower, as pre -existing bearish positioning was 
unwound and Fed speakers (Barkin, Goolsbee) stopped well short 
of signalling an imminent move. This dynamic reinforces our view 
that the technical selling pressure driving yields towards the upper 
end of our range is fading. Structural drivers – term premium, fiscal 
supply concerns and ‘higher for longer’ Fed repricing – remain intact 
and keep us from turning outright bullish on duration. However, our 
opportunistic bullish idea in US 10-year government bonds initiated 
at September-end remains, as we continue to view spikes in US 10-
year yields over 5.25% as a good risk-reward entry. From a longer-
term portfolio standpoint, we continue to favour the 3-7-year tenor. 
On FX, elevated US government bond yields should sustain the 
USD’s interest rate appeal. However, expected hikes alone will not 
drive further appreciation. Stronger data or firmer Fed guidance 
would provide fresh support, while evidence that tightening is 
nearing completion would temper the USD’s strength. 
—  Vincent Tan, Senior Investment Strategist 
— Ray Heung, Senior Investment Strategist

### Page 5

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 16 October 2026

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  What are your key takeaways from US bank earnings? 
Our view: Q3 2026 earnings results from major US banks have 
been mixed, but underlying trends remain encouraging. Healthy 
loan and deposit growth, resilient consumer spending and 
wealth management activity continue to support the sector. 
Credit risks remain manageable. We remain constructive on the 
US financial sector, favouring stronger, diversified franchises. 
Rationale: Healthy loan and deposit growth continue to support net 
interest income (NII), hinting that the US economy remains resilient. 
Capital markets revenue grew year -on-year (y/y) , but moderated 
sequentially after a very strong Q2 2026, although performance 
varied across banks. Management commentary points to a healthy 
deal pipeline , supported by M&A activity and debt refinancing 
demand, although execution will depend on market conditions. 
Provisioning and net charge -off trends were mixed across banks, 
with no clear signs of broad deterioration in credit quality. Firmer 
US inflation raises the risk of further monetary tightening. While 
higher rates can support asset yields, they can also increase 
funding costs, pressure borrowers and weigh on securities ’ 
valuations. We will continue to monitor lending demand, credit 
costs, NII trends, capital markets momentum and capital returns.  
—  Robin Xie, Senior Investment Strategist 
  Can Big Tech’s earnings guidance sustain confidence in 
the AI investment cycle? 
Our view: We expect Big Tech’s AI spending to remain elevated, 
supporting our constructive AI capex outlook. However, we 
await guidance on the pace of capex growth and the 
sustainability of profit margins. 
Rationale: Semiconductor leaders’ latest company guidance points 
to supply constraints extending through 2028, supporting continued 
capacity expansion for compute . However, while AI capex is still 
likely to rise, y/y growth is expected to moderate  from c.100% in 
Q3 2026 to c.20% in Q3 2027. This normalisation in growth could 
raise concerns about the AI investment cycle’s durability.  
The Nasdaq-100 Index has rallied alongside accelerating AI capex. 
Slower capex growth is likely to moderate semiconductor revenue 
growth, while hyperscalers continue to incur depreciation costs on 
past investments. The slower growth is already partially reflected in 
the Nasdaq -100’s valuation de -rating this year. While we remain 
constructive on the AI investment theme, we prefer to stay 
prudent in the near term and await Big Tech’s guidance for greater 
visibility into spending plans in H2 2027 and beyond. 
—  Ryan Goh, Investment Strategist

moderation from a record-high Q2 2026

### Page 6

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 16 October 2026

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  Would France face a credit rating downgrade by rating 
agencies this month? What might be the market implications? 
Our view: A rating downgrade is unlikely unless France’s 2027 
budget passes the National Assembly very soon with material 
changes to the proposal. We see French government bond 
yields remaining elevated relative to German Bund yields. 
Rationale: The French National Assembly began debating France’s 
2027 draft budget on 13 October 2026, with the first vote scheduled 
for 20 October. Near-term budget approval appears unlikely, as 
opposition parties have tabled motions to reject the proposal. The 
French Parliament has up to 70 days from the budget’s submission 
to complete the legislative process, although  its final adoption and 
the resolution of political challenges could extend into early 2027.  
Last October, two of the three major rating agencies downgraded 
France by one notch to the high-single-A category  with stable 
outlooks. The remaining agenc y rated France in the low-AA 
category with a negative outlook. As negative outlooks are typically 
resolved within 12 -18 months, agenc ies have time to await fiscal 
clarity before acting. We believe downgrade risks are largely priced 
in after the recent rise in yields, but volatility is likely to persist amid 
fiscal uncertainty and the approaching presidential election. Banks 
and domestically-focused issuers would face the greatest near-term 
pressure. However, we would view wider spreads as an opportunity 
to add exposure, particularly to French banks’ subordinated 
debt, given their stronger balance sheets and the fact that France 
contributes less than 50% of major French banks’ overall revenue. 
—  Ray Heung, Senior Investment Strategist 
  Are you still positive on Japanese banks? 
Our view: We are positive on Japanese banks despite the recent 
market correction, as the structural drivers of improving 
profitability and shareholder returns remain intact . While 
political risk and shifting BoJ rate hike odds could sustain near-
term volatility, we see the recent pullback as an opportunity to 
build selective exposure to Japanese bank stocks. 
Rationale: Japan’s gradual monetary policy normalisation  
should support banks’ NII as floating-rate loans reprice and maturing 
assets are reinvested at higher yields. Healthy corporate lending 
demand, improving capital efficiency and continued share buybacks 
provide further support . While uncertainty over the pace of BoJ 
tightening could weigh on sentiment, we see gradual interest rate 
normalisation as supportive of medium-term bank profitability.  
Key risks  include rising deposit costs, mark -to-market losses on 
bond portfolios and a disorderly increase in Japanese government 
bond yields. We continue to favour well-capitalised banks  with 
strong domestic deposit franchises and improving returns on equity. 
—  Robin Xie, Senior Investment Strategist

### Page 7

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 16 October 2026

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  Could NZD/USD rebound from its one-year trough? If so, 
how far could it recover?  
Our view: New Zealand’s improving domestic conditions, 
persistent inflation and technical support provide a basis for 
NZD/USD’s initial recovery towards the 0.57 level, although 
elevated US bond yields could slow its progress. 
Rationale: In Q3, New Zealand’s  seasonally-adjusted business 
confidence reading rose to a net 40% of firms expecting the economy 
to improve, up from net 14% in Q2 . Hiring intentions improved, and 
filled jobs rose 0.2% m/m in August. These developments suggest 
domestic conditions remain supportive for the NZD.  
New Zealand’s above-target inflation keeps further RBNZ tightening 
in focus. The 22 October consumer inflation data release and the 
28 October RBNZ policy meeting are key catalysts: persistent price 
pressures a nd improving demand are expected to prompt firmer 
policy guidance and strengthen the NZD’s interest-rate appeal. 
NZD/USD’s position near the lower boundary of its downtrend 
channel suggests scope for a technical rebound. We see 0.57 as 
the first recovery hurdle; a sustained break  above this level  would 
strengthen the case for further gains. Firmer US  consumer inflation 
remains a headwind, making a gradual recovery more plausible 
than a sharp reversal.  
—  Vincent Tan, Senior Investment Strategist 
—  Iris Yuen, Investment Strategist 
  Can the proposed Russia -Ukraine ‘energy ceasefire ’ 
contain oil prices as Middle East risks rise? 
Our view: We expect escalating Middle East tensions to keep oil 
prices elevated . We  maintain our  three- and 12 -month West 
Texas Intermediate (WTI) oil price forecasts at USD 100/bbl and 
USD 80/bbl, respectively, and continue to favour US Treasury 
Inflation-Protected Securities (TIPS) as a portfolio hedge 
against persistent inflation risks. 
Rationale: The proposed Russia-Ukraine ‘energy ceasefire’ still 
looks some way off. In our view, even if an agreement is reached, it 
would do little to ease the supply risks building in the Middle East. 
Gulf oil exports have recovered to above 80% of pre-conflict levels 
in recent weeks, helping moderate the pace of price increases. 
However, the recovery remains vulnerable to disruptions, particularly 
following the latest Houthi attacks on Saudi energy infrastructure . 
Diesel and jet fuel remain under the most pressure , with Gulf 
exports of these fuels in September  at only around 60% of pre -
conflict levels. A sustained disruption to major Saudi production or 
export infrastructure remains the principal upside risk to oil prices. 
—  Anthony Naab, Investment Strategist

## Document 384: The Institute Employment Report_ September 2026 Bank of America Institute analysis shows slower payroll growth, declining unemployment payments and continued strength in lower-inco

Source PDF: all report pdf/The Institute Employment Report_ September 2026 Bank of America Institute analysis shows slower payroll growth, declining unemployment payments and continued strength in lower-inco_223e3a6a.pdf
Report source: Bank of America
Date: 2026-10-01
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

13025075     1

Economy 
The Institute Employment Report: September 2026 
07 October 2026

Key takeaways 
• Job growth softened further in September, according to Bank of America's deposit data, with growth of 1.4% year-over-year 
(YoY), from 1.5% in August. But falling unemployment payments in September indicate a relatively healthy overall picture. 
• Lower-income households' wage growth remains strongest, despite a narrowing gap. After-tax wage growth eased to 4.5% YoY 
for lower-income households in September, while rising to 3.8% and 3.7% for middle- and higher-income households, 
respectively. 
• Lower tax withholdings and a relative strengthening in the younger/lower-income end of the labor market are driving this story. 
Additionally, average hours have risen in some lower-paid industries, which is also boosting pay packets.

Job growth slowed in September, but unemployment trends are benign  
Our estimate of job growth – which uses Bank of America deposit data to track changes in the number of customer accounts 
receiving a paycheck (see Methodology) – slowed further in September, to 1.4% year-over-year (YoY) from 1.5% YoY in August. 
The data can be noisy, partly due to seasonal variation and differences in pay-period timing, but in our view, the latest reading is 
consistent with the easing in payrolls growth seen in the September Bureau of Labor Statistics (BLS) data (Exhibit 1). 
Still, despite slower hiring, there is little sign of a rise in joblessness in Bank of America data. Unemployment payments into 
customer accounts showed a YoY decline in September (Exhibit 2). So, overall, labor market trends appear resilient.   
Exhibit 1: September payroll growth showed further slowing in 
Bank of America deposit account data

(BLS) and Automatic Data Processing (ADP) (monthly, YoY)

### Page 2

After-tax wage growth cooled for lower-income, but rose for other cohorts 
Lower-income wage growth eased to 4.5% YoY in September from 4.7% YoY in August, while wage growth for middle- and 
higher-income households rose to 3.8% and 3.7% YoY, respectively. Overall, these changes were modest, and lower-income 
households are continuing to see faster after-tax wage growth than other cohorts, a notable turnaround from the “K-shaped” 
pattern we observed over much of 2025 and 2026 (Exhibit 3). 
Exhibit 3: Lower-income wage growth remains strongest, despite a narrowing gap 
After-tax wage and salary growth by household income terciles, based on Bank of America aggregated consumer deposit account data (three-month moving 
average, YoY%, SA)

Source: Bank of America internal data 
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE 
In our view, the pick-up in lower- and middle-income households’ after-tax wage growth continues to reflect the impact of the 
One Big Beautiful Bill Act (OBBA) in reducing tax withholdings, alongside an underlying rise in job-to-job changes at the 
younger/lower-income end of the labor market (for more, read: The Institute Employment Report: August 2026). Consistent with 
that underlying pick up, BLS data shows a solid YoY rise in average hours worked in several relatively lower-paid industries, such 
as manufacturing, retail and other services. Hourly workers in these industries are likely seeing rising paychecks as a result 
(Exhibit 4).

Methodology  
Selected Bank of America transaction data is used to inform the macroeconomic views expressed in this report and should be considered in the 
context of other economic indicators and publicly available information. In certain instances, the data may provide directional and/or predictive 
value. The data used is not comprehensive; it is based on aggregated and anonymized selections of Bank of America data and may reflect a 
degree of selection bias and limitations on the data available. 
Any payments data represents aggregated spend from US Retail, Preferred, Small Business and Wealth Management clients with a deposit 
account or credit card. Aggregated spend include total credit card, debit card, ACH, wires, bill pay, business/peer-to-peer, cash, and checks.  
Any Small Business payments data represents aggregate spend from Small Business clients with a deposit account or a Small Business credit 
card. Payroll payments data include channels such as ACH (automated clearing house), bill pay, checks and wire. Bank of America per Small 
Business client data represents activity spending from active Small Business clients with a deposit account or a Small Business credit card and 
at least one transaction in each month. Small businesses in this report include business clients within Bank of America and generally defined as 
under $5mm in annual sales revenue.  
Unless otherwise stated, data is not adjusted for seasonality, processing days or portfolio changes, and may be subject to periodic revisions.  
Bank of America aggregated credit/debit card spending per household includes spending from active US households only. Only consumer card 
holders making a minimum of five transactions a month are included in the dataset. Spending from corporate cards are excluded. Data 
regarding merchants who receive payments are identified and classified by the Merchant Categorization Code (MCC) defined by financial 
services companies. The data are mapped using proprietary methods from the MCCs to the North American Industry Classification System 
(NAICS), which is also used by the Census Bureau, in order to classify spending data by subsector. Spending data may also be classified by other 
proprietary methods not using MCCs.  
Lower, middle and higher household income cuts in Bank of America credit and debit card spending per household, and consumer deposit 
account data are based on quantitative estimates of each households’ income. These quantitative estimates are bucketed according to terciles, 
with a third of households placed in each tercile periodically. The lowest tercile represents ‘lower income’, the middle tercile represents ‘middle 
income’ and the highest tercile ‘higher income’. The income thresholds between these terciles will move over time, reflecting any number of 
factors that impact income, including general wage inflation, changes in social security payments and individual households’ income. The 
income and tercile in which a household is categorised are periodically re-assessed.  
Generations, if discussed, are defined as follows: Gen Z, born after 1995; Younger Millennials: born between 1989-1995; Older Millennials: born 
between 1978-1988; Gen Xers: born between 1965-1977; Baby Boomer: 1946-1964; Traditionalists: pre-1946. 
Estimate of payroll growth from Bank of America internal data is based on the change in customer accounts receiving a paycheck in the month. 
An adjustment is made for the difference between overall population growth and customer account growth. Occasionally historic payments can 
be reclassified, leading to revisions in the series.  
An estimate of bonus growth from Bank of America deposit data is calculated by looking at customers who have received an inbound ACH 
payroll transaction in the last two years. From this sample an estimate of bonuses is derived by looking for payroll transactions which are over 
50% higher than the median regular payroll payments received by the customer. Of these payments only those that were received around the 
same time in each of the last two years are selected.  
The job-to-job change rate (j2j rate) is defined as the proportion of customers with an identified change in their employer as a proportion of 
the total number of customers with employment income. We estimate the median pay rise associated with a j2j change using the pay in the 
first three months of the new job compared to the same three months a year ago. 
Additional information about the methodology used to aggregate the data is available upon request.

### Page 2

The Week Ahead October 2, 2026 
                                                                                                                                         2

CENTRAL BANK MEETINGS

India: RBI monetary policy committee 
meeting (7-Oct)

The macro environment has undergone 
significant changes since the RBI 
monetary policy committee’s (MPC) 
August policy meeting, with global oil 
prices rising past $100bl, and US Fed 
kickstarting their rate hike cycle. 
Domestically, core and headline 
continued to rise, laying the ground for 
inflation to quicken beyond 5.8 -6.0% in 
the second half as price risks broaden. 
Strong 1QFY27 GDP growth meanwhile 
pointed to the economy’s relative 
resilience to external shocks.

Besides fuel feeding into food costs, 
weather has also proved to be disruptive, 
with indications that the El Niño 
phenomenon could extend into 2027. 
International food benchmarks, including 
the UN Food Price index and the 
Bloomberg spot agricultural indice s, 
continue to climb. India’s food inflation 
has become more broad -based, shifting 
beyond vegetables and pulses to sugar, 
milk, protein groups, and edible oils. Sub-
par weather has contributed to this 
upmove, besides idiosyncratic factors 
like swing in production trends (cobweb), 
diversion for fuel usage etc.

After two years of normal monsoon, 
cumulative southwest rainfall this year is 
set to end with rainfall around 12 -13% 
below the long -term average, the most 
since 2015 and resulting in a slower 
build-up in reservoir levels.  
Global benchmark oil price gauge has 
averaged ~$93pb in FY27 year -to-date, 
up 36% yoy  vs FY26. Retail pump fuel 
prices were adjusted in quick succession 
in May26 and have been left unchanged 
since then, while other fuel products 
including CNG, aviation fuel, LPG etc 
have continued to climb. Add to this, 
other pockets like telecom, restaura nts, 
business services, transport fares etc 
have been inching up, reflecting early 
signs of broadening in price pressures (on 
sequential basis). A gradual broadening 
of price pressures is likely to keep 
headline inflation high and narrow the 
real rate buffer significantly.

Authorities are also conducting liquidity 
absorption measures through market 
operations like VRRR, open market 
operations and sell-buy FX swaps. This is 
likely to align the overnight call money 
rates to the benchmark repo rate, after 
having fallen sharply d ue to the surplus 
in the banking system.  Liquidity and core 
inflation have historically displayed a 
modest positive relationship, as per our 
study. As such, the RBI's inflation 
mandate has likely reinforced the push to 
drain excess liquidity.

We expect the RBI MPC to raise the repo 
rate by 25bp to 5.5% , citing pipeline 
inflationary pressures. Stance could be 
changed to withdrawal of 
accommodation to signal hawkish intent. 
We expect one more hike within 4Q26, 
though the odds of additional hikes have 
risen as oil prices stay stubbornly high 
and weather remains unfavourable.

FORTHCOMING DATA 
RELEASES

Malaysia

We expect industrial production to grow 
by 5.8% yoy in August from 4.7% yoy in 
July. Strength in  export-oriented 
manufacturing is likely to persist , 
supported by sustained demand for 
artificial intelligence-related electronics 
goods, while mining output may have 
recovered from its contraction in July.

Taiwan

September trade and inflation data are 
forthcoming. Export growth is expected 
to sustain the 40% pace seen in the 
previous month. The global AI boom is 
facing rising headwinds from 
synchronised rate hikes by G3 central 
banks, higher bond yields, and rising  
financing costs. That said, there are so 
far no clear signs of a significant 
slowdown, based on the high -frequency 
indicators we track across AI demand, 
investment, and supply.

CPI is expected to rebound to 2.4% YoY 
in September, up from 2.0% in August. 
The earlier arrival of the Mid -Autumn 
Festival this year may have boosted food 
and services prices on a YoY basis. The 
rebound in global energy prices should 
also have lifted tran sport costs, 
including gasoline prices and airfares. 
While electricity tariffs will remain 
frozen, the December review implies a 
possibility of an electricity price hike 
after the local elections.

Given still -strong economic growth, 
persistent inflation, and the potential for 
inflation expectations to rise, we 
maintain our view that the central bank 
will raise the policy rate by a modest 
12.5bps to 2.125% in December.

Thailand

We expect Thailand’s headline inflation 
to accelerate to 3.2% yoy in September 
from 2.5% yoy  in August, slightly 
exceeding the central bank’s 1 -3% 
inflation target. Faster increases in 
energy and food prices likely pushed 
overall inflation higher, despite an 
easing in electricity tariffs starting 
September. We expect the Bank of 
Thailand (BOT), w hich views the rise in 
inflation as largely supply -driven, to 
keep its policy rate stable at 1.00% for 
the remainder of 2026, aiming to support 
an economy with low and uneven growth.

漢磊 3707 TT  ( 電子–半導體 ) 
AI 挹注，終迎春燕 
投資與評價 
1. AI 需求強勁，2H26 逐步復甦，SiC 競爭壓力減緩，去庫存亦已完成，
預估 2026 年營收年增 29.0%。 
2. 新增產能陸續開出，應用多點開花，產品價量齊升，預估 2027 年營收
年增 23.8%。 
3. 預估漢磊 2026/2027 年 EPS 為 0.99/4.77 元，考量整體產業趨勢顯著
下，以同業本益比區間中間偏上位置 21 倍為評價基礎(21X FY27E 
EPS)，目標價 100 元，由中立轉為買進投資建議。 
重點更新 
2026 年 Si 逐步回溫，化合物半導體需求跳升，預估營收年增 29.0% 
根據調研，漢磊在六吋需求與產能利用率均有改善，四、五吋廠過去產能利
用率長期偏低，包含原物料取得不易，8 月已停止生產，10 月完成設備與人
員轉移，將資源集中於高價值的六吋與八吋產品，在Si 產品方面，TVS 受惠
AI 伺服器保護電路需求增加及轉單效應， 目前產能處於滿載狀態(使用六吋)，
推估 2H26 較 1H26 成長約 32%，化合物半導體部分，GaN 亦受惠 AI 資料
中心需求，產能滿載，HoH 研究部推估成長 26%，目前客戶需求已達可供應
量的兩倍水準，SiC 部分先前因大陸廠商競爭激烈，亦面臨去庫存，產能利用
率上半年僅約三成水準，現因其他廠商減產以及去庫存完成，3Q26 已回升
至六成，研究部預估 4Q26 將進一步回升至 65%水準，低基期下 2H26 營收
HoH 將成長約 123.5%，整體預估 2026 年營收至年增 29.0%。 
AI 帶動 2027 年維持高速成長，預估營收年增 23.8% 
TVS 部分由於新增產能陸續開出，加上 AI 伺服器、高速介面及晶片微縮後增
加的靜電保護需求，預估 2027 年將成長 19.8%，GaN 應用由消費性快充擴
大至 AI 伺服器 PSU、48V／800V 直流電源轉換架構，考量生產效率以及供
應能力，在客戶需求維持強勁下，研究部預估 2027 年成長約 15%水準，至
於 SiC 應用由電動車主驅逆變器，延伸至車載充電器，AI 資料中心的高功率
PSU 與固態變壓器部分客戶已在開發相關產品，在基期低下，明年 SiC 營收
相較今年將翻倍成長，目前與世界先進合作的八吋的月產能約 1,500 片，漢
磊自有六吋 SiC 月產能約 5,000 片，合計約 8,000 片六吋等效月產能。整體
2027 年研究部預估營收將年增 23.75%，達 92.1 億元。

### Page 2

訪談內容 
SiC 合作優於預期，需求逐步爬坡 
SiC 目前 AI 伺服器功率需求逾 3kW 至 8kW 以上，SiC MOSFET 用於 3kW 以上 PSU，可縮小
體積及降低散熱成本， 另外太陽能逆變器 、 儲能與其他高壓系統逐步由650V、750V 往 1200V、
1700V 及更高電壓發展，漢磊客戶已有 3300V 產品，SiC 在價格達到甜蜜點下亦往消費性電子
及小家電滲透。 
而與世界先進合作的八吋 SiC 設備已全數到位，首批試產片良率優於預期，目標年底前進入小量
量產，首波五個客戶準備 T ape-out，預估 4Q26 至 1Q27 陸續推進，漢磊負責客戶與技術平台 ，
將技術導入合作產線，世界先進提供生產線及製造服務，客戶關係及技術仍由漢磊負責。八吋目
前預估月產能約 1,500 片，漢磊自有六吋 SiC 月產能約 5,000 片，八吋面積約為六吋的 1.7~1.8
倍，良率也較高，估算八吋1,500 片約相當於六吋 3,000 片，初期合計約8,000 片六吋等效月產
能，透過較小規模投資，合作八吋產線有機會擴至月產 3,000 片，合計六吋等效月產能可超過 1
萬片。

表一、漢磊 2026 年修正預估與前次預估比較 
資料來源：國泰證期研究部預估

### Page 3

漢磊 ESG 政策 
漢磊主要供應元件及積體電路代工事業：雙載子積體電路(Bipolar IC Service)、高功率場效電晶
體(Power MOSFET)、混合型積體電路(邏輯元件)、高壓 CMOS、高壓 BCD、GaN 及 SiC 分離式
元件等。產業類別歸類在半導體行業中，依據專門為投資人設計的永續揭露框架 SASB 準則，半
導體行業關注重點為下列幾項：溫室氣體排放、能源管理、水和廢水管理、廢棄物和危險材料管
理、員工健康與安全、員工多元化和包容性、產品設計與生命週期管理、材料採購與效率。因漢
磊企業社會責任報告書尚未更新至 2021 年以及 2020 年，相關資訊揭露較少，故從已知資訊作
分析。

1. 節省能源消耗政策： 
漢磊所使用於辦公室及生產廠區的能源，包含外購電力及各類化石燃料相關統計如下： 
a. 天然氣：主要用於有機廢氣吸附及燃燒處理系統與餐廳。 
b. 柴油：主要用於緊急發電機，當停電、定期保養及緊急狀況時才啟動，非工廠生產所需直接能
源。 
c. 外購電力：提供公司內部照明、空調、動力等的系統運作。 
因應產品特性 ， 新增6 吋 SiC 製程產線及車用產品品質要求 ，2019 年單位用電量較前 2 年增加，
但總用電量仍呈現下降趨勢。能源的使用不僅會消耗地球之資源，同時亦會產生二氧化碳，造成
溫室效應。漢磊科歷年來持續進行各項節能技術，並進行相關設施之節能改善計畫，節能成效顯
著。

圖一、漢磊節能策略

資料來源:漢磊、國泰證期研究部整理

2. 水資源管理及水汙染防治： 
漢磊用水均來自於自來水廠供應 ， 對水源並無顯著影響 。 其主要提供製程 、 空調及民生消耗所需 。
2019 年因應產品特性，新增 6 吋 SiC 製程產線及車用產品品質要求，單位用水量較前 2 年增，
但總用水量仍呈現下降趨勢。受全球氣候變遷影響，漢磊科更積極推動節能減碳活動，除了積極
節水外，也持續推動節水專案及引進各方面節水技術，2019 年節水比率逐步成長至 10.19%。漢
磊科廠區之用水量除蒸發損耗及綠化用水外 ， 其餘生活廢水及製程循環廢水皆經由適當的污水處
理設施處理後 ， 排入污水下水道 ， 流入管理局廢水處理廠 。 廢水排放水質皆符合主管機關之要求 ，
對生態環境及當地社區無顯著衝擊。

### Page 4

圖二、漢磊節水策略

資料來源:漢磊、國泰證期研究部整理

圖三、漢磊 2017~2019 年用水回收率

資料來源:漢磊、國泰證期研究部整理

3. 碳排放管理： 
漢磊廠內配合國家溫室氣體減量法推動及相關政策致力於降低溫室氣體的衝擊。依循 CNS 
14064-1 
及行政院環保署溫室氣體查驗指引、溫室氣體排放量盤查登錄作業指引，並撰寫溫室氣體盤查報
告 書 ， 對 外 揭 露 溫 室 氣 體 排 放 量 數 據。 半 導 體 廠 的 主 要 溫 室 氣 體 來 源 為 全 氟 化 物
（Perfluorocompound, PFC），以≤ 150mm (六吋)晶圓最佳之技術選擇是製程最佳化（Process 
Optimization） 及取代化學品 （Alternative Chemistries） 方式進行PFC 排放減量。另外漢磊之
溫室氣體排放主要為使用電力間接排放之溫室氣體約佔總排放量之 58.87%，對溫室氣體減量的
努力將著重於用電設備能源效率之提升。2019 年溫室氣體增加主要是因應產能提升需求，採購
新機器設備而至用電量增加，故溫室氣體排放量較基準年高。

圖四、溫室氣體統計圖

資料來源:漢磊、國泰證期研究部整理

### Page 5

4. 廢棄物管理： 
漢磊 2019 年產出廢棄物數量為 566 噸 ， 較2018 年減少 26 噸 ， 其中回收及再利用廢棄物為110
噸佔總量 19%； 一般事業廢棄物為199 噸佔總量 35%； 有害事業廢棄物為258 噸佔總量 46%。
將廠區廢棄物種類進行分析，2019 年回收及再利用部分較 2018 年減少 13%，一般事業廢棄物
則減少 4%，有害事業廢棄物則增加 16%，當中 42%為專管回收有害高濃度氨氮廢液，降低環境
廢水的污染，並更有效率的使廢棄物再進入生產鏈的循環中。因應環保理念，漢磊本著永續經營
的理念與遵守法規之要求進行廢棄物妥善處理(如.將原本以廢水處理系統處理的含氟廢液，轉為
回收及再利用方式處理)，以避免生產活動造成對環境之衝擊。

緯穎 6669 TT(電子–電腦及週邊設備) 
GPU、ASIC 並進，27 年獲利創高 
投資與評價 
1. 因缺料影響降低，加上 Trainium3 開始出貨，緯穎 3Q26 營收優於預期
17%，上修獲利 25%至 EPS 43.9 元，3Q26 營收、獲利將同創新高。 
2. 在 ASIC、GPU 貢獻下，4Q26 營收估季增高個位數 ，AI 比重將逾 55~60%，
因 2H26 優於預期，上修今年營收 8%至 1.46 兆元，EPS 140.3 元。 
3. AMD Helios 為緯穎 27 年 AI 強勁動能，加上公司為 Trainium 水冷產品主
供，帶動 27 年稅後獲利 YoY+60%，CB 對股權稀釋僅 1.03%，EPS 估續
創新高達 224.71 元，維持買進評等，目標價 2,400 元。 
重點更新 
3Q26 營收、獲利上修，反映一般伺服器雙位數季增及液冷 ASIC 加入貢獻 
緯穎 3Q26 營收 4,346 億元，QoQ+56%、YoY+63%，優於研究部預期 17%，
主要因缺料影響較預期低，加上 9 月 AWS Trainium3 開始出貨，因本季一般伺
服器占比超越 50%，估毛利率維持 8.53%，稅後獲利上修 25%至 245.95 億元，
YoY+59%至 EPS 43.9 元。預期 3Q26 營收、獲利將同創新高。  
4Q26 AI 比重攀升至 55%以上，ASIC、GPU 同步貢獻，上修今年營收 8% 
研究部估緯穎 4Q26 營收將季增高個位數，AI 部分，ASIC 出貨 Trainium3 液冷
貢獻，ASP 估較氣冷版翻倍成長；此外，GPU 部分，預期 AMD Helios 及 Oracle 
Vera Rubin 皆有少量出貨，加上缺料持續改善，估 AI 比重將超越 55~60%，使
毛利率下滑至 8.2%，稅後獲利 YoY+81%，EPS 44.5 元。因 2H26 狀況優於預
期，上修今年營收 8%至 1.46 兆元 ，YoY+54%，反映下半年 AI 比重攀升至 55%，
毛利率 8.38%，稅後獲利 YoY+54%，EPS 140.34 元。 
27 年營運在 ASIC、GPU 成長增速下，獲利將再創新高 
展望 2027 年，預期 AMD Helios 將為 AI 強勁動能，2027 年 AMD GPU 機櫃估
達五到六千櫃 ，ASP 500 萬美元 ， 加上VR 機櫃貢獻 ， 估使GPU 占比拉升至 30%。
ASIC 部分，我們認為緯穎仍是 Trainium 水冷產品主供，預期 AWS 水冷比重大
幅攀升，估AWS 營收年增四到五成；一般伺服器維持兩成以上增長。綜上，上修
27 年營收 11%至 2.64 兆元，YoY+80.4 %，因 AI 占比估達六到七成，估毛利率
下滑至 7.35%，稅後獲利 YoY+60%，EPS 224.71 元，獲利續創新高。籌資部分，
緯穎將於 11 月完成 150 億元國內可轉債發行，預計在 1Q27~2Q27 分別使用 
80 億元、70.75 億元，對於整體股權稀釋僅 1.03%，影響有限。

【市場短評】
受投資人低檔承接 ，美債殖利率從高檔回落 ，使金價從低
檔反彈 ，然受中東緊張情勢恐推升通膨 、消費者及生產者
物價數據高於預期 ，壓低金價 ，但美聯準會官員態度多認
為無升息急迫性 ，年底前才可能再升息的偏鷹言論 ，緩解
對10月升息預期 ，使美元及美債殖利率回落 ，支撐金價 ，
累計本週國際金價上漲 0.9%。下周關注美國製造業 PMI初
值、初請失業金人數及中東地緣緊張對油價的影響 ，預估
短期受偏高的債券殖利率及美元走強相對不利金價走勢 。
但若美債殖利率回落 、擔憂債務可持續性 、分散交易對手
風險及多元化資產配置 ，中國人行連續 23個月購金 ，並加
大購金步伐，預期中期金價仍有機會緩升。

证券研究报告 | 宏观点评

宏观点评 
 【粤开宏观】前三季度经济形势快评 
2026 年 10 月 19 日  投资要点

近期报告  
《 【粤开宏观】9 月出口再提速： 涨价支撑

征、问题与制度成因》2026-10-07

美国对华关税的“变”与“不变”》2026-09-27 
《 【粤开宏观】对解决地方财政问题的思
考：兼谈地方附加税的意义》2026-09-21

一、如何看待前三季度经济总体形势？ 
可以从亮点成绩、存在问题及解决对策出发，并将之置于全球经济环境下分
析。 
第一，当前全球经济仍处于“低增长、高通胀”的总体态势，全球性的贸易
摩擦、老龄化、贫富分化抑制了出口、投资和消费活动，制约了全球经济的
低增长；同时 AI 带来的资本开支和财富效益是新的增量。 
第二，当然还存在一系列亟待问题的问题，包括需求不足尤其是消费不足、
企业面临的拖欠账款影响经济循环、房地产市场仍在持续调整以及由此带来
的地方财政收支紧平衡。 
第三，国庆前后一系列增量政策陆续出台，伴随前期六张网建设部署以及新
型政策性金融工具，预计能较好稳定经济增长，当然最核心还是要通过改革
和政策来激发微观主体积极性，包括收入分配、财税体制、市场准入和产权
保护改革等。 
二、如何看待前三季度投资增速放缓？ 
当前基建投资增速放缓是提高投资效益背景下的主动调整以及优质项目相对
稀缺的共同结果，这是新旧动能转换过程中必经阶段，经济转型意味着要从
传统基建投资驱动转向新质生产力驱动、从投资于物转向投资于人于人，这
就导致传统基建的数据相对下行；在看到投资增速下行的同时也要看到投资
结构的优化，投资结构的优化有利于提高财政资金使用效益以及降低债务风
险。 
三、政策建议：从宏观数据导向到微观感受导向，着眼于三大主体 
理念层面，要坚持“耐心政策”，推动宏观政策从注重 GDP、投资等宏观数据
的短期改善， 转向更加注重居民、 企业和地方政府三大微观主体的实际感受。 
操作层面，要围绕居民、企业和地方政府三大主体，从六个方面着力改善收
入预期、经营环境和发展能力。

风险提示：宏观政策超预期、外部冲击超预期

### Page 3

宏观点评
请务必阅读最后特别声明与免责条款 www.ykzq.com  3 / 6
一、如何看待前三季度经济总体形势？
可以从亮点成绩、存在问题及解决对策出发，并将之置于全球经济环境下分析。
第一，当前全球经济仍处于“低增长、高通胀”的总体态势，全球性的贸易摩擦、
老龄化、贫富分化抑制了出口、投资和消费活动，制约了全球经济的低增长；同时 AI
带来的资本开支和财富效益是新的增量。在逆全球化、保护主义和产业链重构背景下，
中国经济依然保持较好的韧性， 当然也有进一步要解决的问题。 中国前三季度GDP 同比
实现 4.6%，名义增长 5.6%，尤其是三季度还较二季度回升0.2 个百分点，从宏观数据看
保持了较强韧性。 这种韧性源自出口和以AI 为代表的新质生产力的拉动作用。 近年来受
出口市场多元化和出口产品升级支撑，AI 贡献了出口增速的一半，带动的集成电路出口
和计算机生产和投资都增速较好； 同时经济结构上知识产权投资、 高技术投资增速较好。
1-9 月份知识产权产品投资同比增长 9.3%，高技术产业投资同比增长 4.9%。

第二，当然还存在一系列亟待问题的问题，包括需求不足尤其是消费不足、企业面
临的拖欠账款影响经济循环、房地产市场仍在持续调整以及由此带来的地方财政收支紧
平衡。 消费不足需要下大力气解决收入分配和提高社会保障水平， 有必要提高育儿补贴、
提高城乡居民养老金水平等、优化服务供给、优化支持消费政策，从支持商品为主到服
务为主；企业拖欠账款问题，要从源头上解决，包括继续提高政府债务限额或者增加转
移支付来提高其偿债能力。
第三，国庆前后一系列增量政策陆续出台，伴随前期六张网建设部署以及新型政策
性金融工具，预计能较好稳定经济增长，当然最核心还是要通过改革和政策来激发微观
主体积极性，包括收入分配、财税体制、市场准入和产权保护改革等。
二、如何看待前三季度投资增速放缓？
当前基建投资增速放缓是提高投资效益背景下的主动调整以及优质项目相对稀缺的
共同结果，这是新旧动能转换过程中必经阶段，经济转型意味着要从传统基建投资驱动
转向新质生产力驱动、从投资于物转向投资于人于人，这就导致传统基建的数据相对下
行；在看到投资增速下行的同时也要看到投资结构的优化，投资结构的优化有利于提高

财政资金使用效益以及降低债务风险。
具体而言，重大项目建设更加注重质量和资金效益，专项债“自审自发”试点扩围
后减少国家发改委审核环节后地方政府的审核反而更加审慎和严格，压实了地方责任，
项目从储备到开工周期有所延长，这是财政管理从“重规模”向“重效益”转变的积极
信号。
四季度基建投资有望边际回升，全年将呈现“前低后高”走势。从项目端看，算力
网、能源网、水网等“六张网”重点工程完成前期准备后逐步转入建设实施阶段，地方
政府前期储备项目开始转入集中实施阶段。从资金端看，四季度专项债发行规模有望加
大，叠加盘活地方债结存限额用于支持新增投资项目、政策性金融工具资金投放政策密
集落地，四季度基建投资降幅有望较前三季度收窄。
总体来看，我国在交通、能源、水利、城市更新等领域仍有较大投资空间，随着投
融资体制改革进一步深化，基建投资将回归平稳增长轨道。

### Page 4

宏观点评
请务必阅读最后特别声明与免责条款 www.ykzq.com  4 / 6
三、 政策建议： 从宏观数据导向到微观感受导向， 着眼于三大
主体
理念层面，要坚持“耐心政策”，推动宏观政策从注重 GDP、投资等宏观数据的短期
改善，转向更加注重居民、企业和地方政府三大微观主体的实际感受，通过持续深化居
民部门的收入分配与社保改革、企业部门的保护产权和市场准入放开、地方政府的财税
制度改革，稳定预期、激发经济内生增长动力。短期增量政策虽然能够改善经济数据，
但难以从根本上解决消费不足、企业信心偏弱和地方财政承压等深层次问题，必须更加
重视政策的长期性、连续性和微观获得感。
操作层面，要围绕居民、企业和地方政府三大主体，从六方面着力改善收入预期、
经营环境和发展能力。一是优化以旧换新政策， 扩围至服务业。 二是优化国民收入分配，
提高居民收入占比：“国资-财政-社保”改革； 提高上市公司分红； 建立城乡居民增收引导
基金。三是推动住房公积金定位从房地产建设和消费储备基金，转为广义民生基金（大

病医疗/代际互助/消费提取） 。四是增发 2-3 万亿国债并转移支付地方政府，恢复地方政
府发展经济的能力和积极性。五是增发国债成立房地产稳定基金，用于收地与应对房地
产风险。六是继续降息，推动房贷利率低于租金收益率。

### Page 5

宏观点评
